# SNAPSHOT_MANIFEST -- trading-system-sbx-snapshot

> **THIS REPOSITORY IS A READ-ONLY SNAPSHOT OF FILES COPIED FROM A MACHINE. IT IS NEVER A DEPLOY TARGET.**
> Never push it to any VM, bare repository or deploy path. It exists so a rebuild can be designed against what was actually running.

## 1. Machine and time
- **Machine:** the TESTING VM `trading-sbx` -- 130.210.13.114 (hostname `trading-system-sandbox`), user `ubuntu`, tree `/home/ubuntu/systems/trading-system`.
- **Captured:** 17-Sep-2026 (Thursday), IST -- credential scan 20:03:06-20:03:28 IST; file stream 20:06:07-20:06:28 IST. The trading service was stopped (inactive since its own 17:35:04 shutdown).
- **Captured by:** Claude Code (model `claude-opus-5[1m]`) on the PC, read-only on the VM: a `tar` stream to standard output and in-memory reads. Nothing was written on the VM.

## 2. The state the files were in
- There is **no `.git` in the deployed tree** -- the running files are at no commit.
- The machine's bare repository `/home/ubuntu/trading-system.git` has `main` = `20061b6`. **It does NOT describe these files.**
- The files are: the `970aabf`-based tree + the 17 files of the local, never-pushed commit `e7bf477` (S&R shadow v1.3, delivered 16-Sep-2026) + the `force_qty` change delivered 17-Sep-2026 19:31 IST (`core/config_loader.py` md5 `2030714e78d55b0a8a4ea8db58a987ae` · `capital/position_sizer.py` `b2b27684244691d2fdcd15eae7d97837` · `main.py` `277b07028d5e265aab74ce026787ecc8` · `config/system_config.yaml` `12d79b2a03a4becd989baae56078e058`) + VM-local configuration + CRLF-only variants of some files.
- **Byte-exact:** line-ending conversion is disabled for this repository (`.git/info/attributes`: `* -text`); every committed blob equals the VM's bytes (sha256 verified file by file). File modes: executable bits copied from the VM.

## 3. Contents
- **1395 files** including this manifest; **23,966,042 bytes** before this manifest.
- `_machine/` -- machine state that lives OUTSIDE the tree, captured as text:
  - `_machine/bare-repo-hooks/post-receive`
  - `_machine/bare-repo-hooks/post-receive.pre-cron-framework.bak`
  - `_machine/crontab_ubuntu.txt`
  - `_machine/systemd/alert-watcher.service.txt`
  - `_machine/systemd/cron-watchdog.service.txt`
  - `_machine/systemd/cron-watchdog.timer.txt`
  - `_machine/systemd/gui-dashboard.service.txt`
  - `_machine/systemd/security-watcher.service.txt`
  - `_machine/systemd/token-watcher.service.txt`
  - `_machine/systemd/trading-system.service.txt`
  - `_machine/systemd/trading-watchman.service.txt`
  (`crontab_ubuntu.txt` = `crontab -l`; `systemd/*.txt` = `systemctl cat <unit>` incl. drop-ins; `bare-repo-hooks/` = the hooks INSTALLED in the bare repository, which may differ from `deploy/hooks/`.)
- **Kept IN by explicit instruction although they are data files:** `config/instruments.csv` (reference data) · `config/cron_registry.yaml`.

## 4. Credential scan -- values never printed, never copied off the VM
- **VM-side (in memory, on the VM):** 25 distinct secret values from `.env`, two pre-rotation `.env` backups under `/home/ubuntu/preserved/`, `data_store/session/zerodha_token.json` (`access_token`, `api_key`), `data_store/session/gui_secret_key`, plus 14 more values harvested from `/home/ubuntu/.gemini` (2,406 files read) under Zerodha/Telegram/webhook/SMTP key names of several accounts. Shape rules: PEM private keys · Telegram bot tokens · GitHub/AWS/Slack tokens · quoted assignment literals. `.docx`/zip members decompressed and scanned.
- **PC-side (on the extracted copy):** 12 distinct secret values from the PC's `.env`; the same shape rules; set equality (no file missing or extra) and sha256 of every file equal to the VM-scanned bytes.
- **Positive controls -- all fired:** every credential source detects its own values; synthetic PEM / Telegram / GitHub / assignment strings detected; a secret inside a zip member detected; a real value embedded mid-text detected.
- **Result -- 6 files EXCLUDED for credential content** (listed in section 5): 3 carry a real-looking Zerodha API key of account LFL836; 3 carry a real Telegram channel ID (the instruction counts chat IDs as credentials).
- **8 included files match only PLACEHOLDER values** -- a 15-character uppercase placeholder configured for accounts D351962 / ZA004 / ZA005, and a placeholder secondary Telegram channel (verified by value SHAPE and placeholder-word tests, the values themselves never printed):
  - `PATHS.md`
  - `docs/audit/audit_05jul2026.md`
  - `docs/audit/c1_credential_exposure_inventory_02jul2026.md`
  - `docs/audit/system_security_audit_02jul2026.md`
  - `docs/audit/telegram_token_shadow_investigation_03jul2026.md`
  - `docs/decisions/RUNBOOK_2fa_seed_vm_only.md`
  - `docs/web_claude/04_post_build_ops/pending_items_snapshot.txt`
  - `tests/unit/test_secret_scan.py`
- **Quoted-assignment shape matches, judged test placeholders** (low entropy, equal to no known value):
  - `docs/web_claude/02_phase_instructions/consolidated_pre_phase_c.txt` -- bot_token~te..(20)
  - `docs/web_claude/03_audit_responses/deep_system_audit_2026-04-25.md` -- password~ap..(17)
  - `docs/web_claude/03_audit_responses/full_audit_raw_dump.txt` -- bot_token~te..(20)
  - `tests/unit/test_config_loader.py` -- password~ap..(17)|password~ig..(17)
  - `tests/unit/test_config_snapshotter.py` -- password~hu..(21)
  - `tests/unit/test_zerodha_login.py` -- access_token~va..(22)
- `config/system_config.yaml` contains the alert e-mail address (an identifier, not a secret) -- kept.
- **A BLIND SPOT, found and closed before any commit:** the value scan and the quoted-literal rule could NOT see `ops_dashboard/backend/config/gui_config.local.yaml` (a gitignored local file) -- it holds a GUI login password hash and a TOTP secret as UNQUOTED YAML, and neither value exists in any `.env`. Two value-independent rules were added and run over every included file (zip members included), each with a control that fired:
  - **G7 -- unquoted key/value secrets** (key names containing secret / password / pwd / token / api key / totp / seed / private key / credential / hash / salt / cookie / session key / auth, value 12+ characters, obvious non-values skipped): 229 candidate lines; 30 secret-like by shape and entropy -- 2 real (both in the GUI local config, EXCLUDED), the rest code expressions, type names or named test placeholders (incl. the public pyotp documentation example `JBSWY3DPEHPK3PXP`); every low-entropy candidate under a credential key name reviewed by shape -- variable names and test placeholders.
  - **G8 -- bare token shapes with no key name** (32-char base32 TOTP seeds; 32-char lowercase non-hex alphanumerics, so md5 hashes are not flagged; 32-char mixed-case alphanumerics; 16-char lowercase API-key shapes): 67 hits -- 1 real (the same TOTP secret, already excluded); 57 inside two base64 `data:image/webp` URIs in dashboard HTML templates; 8 CamelCase test identifiers; 1 scanner-test fixture.
- **Name-rule false positives, adjudicated:** `docs/audit/critical_alert_stream_audit_25jul2026.md` (a document about alerts, not an alert copy) and `tests/unit/test_gui_secret_key.py` (test code) matched an exclusion NAME pattern; both are code/documentation and scanned clean -- kept.
- **Files the tree's own `.gitignore` hides, added deliberately** (they are machine files and scanned clean): `config/instruments.csv` · `docs/system_manuals/chartink_mounted_strategies.docx` · `docs/system_manuals/config_file_guide.docx` · `docs/system_manuals/trading_System_v2_runbook.docx` · `memory/token_workflow_confirmed_21-Jun-2026.md` · `_machine/bare-repo-hooks/post-receive.pre-cron-framework.bak`.
- **Limits:** the PRODUCTION VM's own `.env` was NOT read (production access not yet approved) -- a credential that exists ONLY there is covered by the shape rules alone. Image content cannot be scanned for credentials -- the screenshots under `docs/` are excluded for that reason.
